/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AnalyzePrototypeProperties.NameInfo.markReference(JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AnalyzePrototypeProperties.java
Original range: 655-679
Documentation:
/**
     * Mark a reference in a given module to this property name, and record
     * the deepest common module reference.
     * @param module The module where it was referenced.
     * @return Whether the name info has changed.
     */
Method code:
655 |     boolean markReference(JSModule module) {
656 |       boolean hasChanged = false;
657 |       if (!referenced) {
658 |         referenced = true;
659 |         hasChanged = true;
660 |       }
661 | 
662 |       if (moduleGraph != null) {
663 |         JSModule originalDeepestCommon = deepestCommonModuleRef;
664 | 
665 |         if (deepestCommonModuleRef == null) {
666 |           deepestCommonModuleRef = module;
667 |         } else {
668 |           deepestCommonModuleRef =
669 |               moduleGraph.getDeepestCommonDependencyInclusive(
670 |                   deepestCommonModuleRef, module);
671 |         }
672 | 
673 |         if (originalDeepestCommon != deepestCommonModuleRef) {
674 |           hasChanged = true;
675 |         }
676 |       }
677 | 
678 |       return hasChanged;
679 |     }

/**
   * Determines whether this node is strictly on the left hand side of an assign

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

===== M0027 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 93-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
93 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
94 |     int cycleCount = 0;
95 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
96 | 
97 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
98 |     // doesn't converge.
99 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
100 | 
101 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
102 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
103 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
104 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
105 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
106 |     }
107 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
108 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
109 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
110 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
111 |       // on the next iteration.
112 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
113 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
114 | 
115 |       workSet.remove(source);
116 | 
117 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
118 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
119 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
120 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
121 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
127 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
128 |   }

91 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
92 |     switch (n.getType()) {
93 |       case Token.LABEL:
94 |         validateLabel(n);
95 |         return;
96 |       case Token.BLOCK:
97 |         validateBlock(n);
98 |         return;
99 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
100 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
101 |         return;
102 |       case Token.WITH:
103 |         validateWith(n);
104 |         return;
105 |       case Token.FOR:
106 |         validateFor(n);
107 |         return;
108 |       case Token.WHILE:
109 |         validateWhile(n);
110 |         return;
111 |       case Token.DO:
112 |         validateDo(n);
113 |         return;
114 |       case Token.SWITCH:
115 |         validateSwitch(n);
116 |         return;
117 |       case Token.IF:
118 |         validateIf(n);
119 |         return;
120 |       case Token.VAR:
121 |         validateVar(n);
122 |         return;
123 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
124 |         validateExprStmt(n);
125 |         return;
126 |       case Token.RETURN:
127 |         validateReturn(n);
128 |         return;
129 |       case Token.THROW:
130 |         validateThrow(n);
131 |         return;
132 |       case Token.TRY:
133 |         validateTry(n);
134 |         return;
135 |       case Token.BREAK:
136 |         validateBreak(n);
137 |         return;
138 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
139 |         validateContinue(n);
140 |         return;
141 |       case Token.EMPTY:
142 |         validateChildless(n);
143 |         return;
144 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
145 |         validateChildless(n);
146 |         return;
147 |       default:
148 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
149 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()) + ".", n);
150 |     }
151 |   }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1412-1431
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1412 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1413 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1414 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1415 |     }
1416 | 
1417 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1418 |     Node n, n2;
1419 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1420 |          res == null && n != null;
1421 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1422 |       if (node2 == null) {
1423 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1424 |       }
1425 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1426 |       if (res != null) {
1427 |         return res;
1428 |       }
1429 |     }
1430 |     return res;
1431 |   }

===== M0056 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

   * Remove all properties under a given name if the property name is
   * never referenced.
   */
Method code:
80 |   private void removeUnusedSymbols(Collection<NameInfo> allNameInfo) {
81 |     boolean changed = false;
82 |     for (NameInfo nameInfo : allNameInfo) {
83 |       if (!nameInfo.isReferenced()) {
84 |         for (Symbol declaration : nameInfo.getDeclarations()) {
85 |           boolean canRemove = false;
86 |

88 |             canRemove = true;
89 |           } else {
90 |             Node specializableFunction =
91 |               getSpecializableFunctionFromSymbol(declaration);
92 | 
93 |             if (specializableFunction != null) {
94 |               specializationState.reportRemovedFunction(
95 |                   specializableFunction, null);
96 |               canRemove = true;
97 |             }
98 |           }
99 | 
100 |           if (canRemove) {
101 |             declaration.remove();
102 |             changed = true;
103 |           }
104 |         }
105 | 
106 |         logger.fine("Removed unused prototype property: " + nameInfo.name);
107 |       }
108 |     }
109 |

111 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
112 |     }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.

843 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {

845 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
846 |       count++;
847 |     }

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 234-277
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",

/**
   * Adds a node at the left-hand side of an expression. Unlike
   * {@link #addExpr(Node,int)}, this preserves information about the context.
   *
   * The left side of an expression is special because in the JavaScript
   * grammar, certain tokens may be parsed differently when they are at
   * the beginning of a statement. For example, "{}" is parsed as a block,
   * but "{'x': 'y'}" is parsed as an object literal.

===== M0082 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

621 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
622 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
623 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
624 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =

Method code:
1067 |   private static void toStringTreeHelper(Node n, int level, Appendable sb)
1068 |       throws IOException {
1069 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
1070 |       for (int i = 0; i != level; ++i) {
1071 |         sb.append("    ");
1072 |       }

===== M0114 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 117-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

Method code:
560 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
561 |     if (child == first) {
562 |       return null;
563 |     }
564 |     Node n = first;
565 |     while (n.next != child) {
566 |       n = n.next;
567 |       if (n == null) {
568 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return n;
572 |   }

===== M0121 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 730-756
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
730 |   @Override
731 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
732 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
733 |       return false;
734 |     }
735 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
736 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
737 |       return false;
738 |     }
739 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
740 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
741 |         return this == that;
742 |       }
743 |       return false;
744 |     }
745 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
747 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
748 |       }
749 |       return false;
750 |     }
751 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
755 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
756 |   }

84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }

97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 |

104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

380 |   private void validateVar(Node n) {
381 |     validateNodeType(Token.VAR, n);
382 |     this.validateMinimumChildCount(n, 1);
383 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
384 |       // Don't use the validateName here as the NAME is allowed to have
385 |       // a child.

391 |       }
392 |     }
393 |   }

314 |     private boolean isPrototypePropertyAssign(Node assign) {
315 |       Node n = assign.getFirstChild();
316 |       if (n != null && NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(n, assign)
317 |           && n.getType() == Token.GETPROP
318 |           ) {
319 |         // We want to exclude the assignment itself from the usage list
320 |         boolean isChainedProperty =
321 |             n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP;
322 | 
323 |         if (isChainedProperty) {
324 |           Node child = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild().getNext();
325 | 
326 |           if (child.getType() == Token.STRING &&
327 |               child.getString().equals("prototype")) {

330 |         }
331 |       }
332 |

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 928-1046
Documentation:

Method code:
928 |   private void toString(
929 |       StringBuilder sb,
930 |       boolean printSource,
931 |       boolean printAnnotations,
932 |       boolean printType) {
933 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
934 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
935 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
936 |         sb.append(' ');
937 |         sb.append(getString());
938 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
939 |         sb.append(' ');
940 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
941 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
942 |         // toStringTree.
943 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
944 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
945 |         } else {
946 |           sb.append(first.getString());
947 |         }
948 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
949 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
950 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
951 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
952 |           sb.append(' ');
953 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
954 |         }
955 |         if (printSource) {
956 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
957 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
958 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
959 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
960 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
961 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
962 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
963 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
964 |           sb.append(']');
965 |         }
966 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
967 |         sb.append(' ');
968 |         sb.append(getDouble());
969 |       }
970 |       if (printSource) {
971 |         int lineno = getLineno();
972 |         if (lineno != -1) {
973 |           sb.append(' ');
974 |           sb.append(lineno);
975 |         }
976 |       }
977 | 
978 |       if (printAnnotations) {
979 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
980 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
981 |           int type = keys[i];
982 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
983 |           sb.append(" [");
984 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
985 |           sb.append(": ");
986 |           String value;
987 |           switch (type) {
988 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
989 |               value = "target block property";
990 |               break;
991 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
992 |               value = "last local block";
993 |               break;
994 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
995 |               switch (x.intValue) {
996 |                 case BOTH:
997 |                   value = "both";
998 |                   break;
999 |                 case RIGHT:
1000 |                   value = "right";
1001 |                   break;
1002 |                 case LEFT:
1003 |                   value = "left";
1004 |                   break;
1005 |                 default:
1006 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1007 |               }
1008 |               break;
1009 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1010 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1011 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1012 |                   value = "eval";
1013 |                   break;
1014 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1015 |                   value = "with";
1016 |                   break;
1017 |                 default:
1018 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1019 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1020 |               }
1021 |               break;
1022 |             default:
1023 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1024 |               if (obj != null) {
1025 |                 value = obj.toString();
1026 |               } else {
1027 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1028 |               }
1029 |               break;
1030 |           }
1031 |           sb.append(value);
1032 |           sb.append(']');
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 | 
1036 |       if (printType) {
1037 |         if (jsType != null) {
1038 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1039 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1040 |             sb.append(" : ");
1041 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1042 |           }
1043 |         }
1044 |       }
1045 |     }
1046 |   }

===== M0191 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1599-1658
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1599 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1600 |     if (type != node.getType()
1601 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1602 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1603 |       return false;
1604 |     }
1605 | 
1606 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1607 |       return false;
1608 |     }
1609 | 
1610 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1611 |       try {
1612 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1613 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1614 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1615 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1616 |             return false;
1617 |           }
1618 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1619 |           return false;
1620 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1621 |           return false;
1622 |         } else {
1623 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1624 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1625 |               return false;
1626 |             }
1627 |           }
1628 |         }
1629 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1630 |         return false;
1631 |       }
1632 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1633 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1634 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1635 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1636 |         return false;
1637 |       }
1638 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1639 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1640 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1641 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1642 |         return false;
1643 |       }
1644 |     }
1645 | 
1646 |     if (recurse) {
1647 |       Node n, n2;
1648 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1649 |            n != null;
1650 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1651 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1652 |           return false;
1653 |         }
1654 |       }
1655 |     }
1656 | 
1657 |     return true;
1658 |   }

===== M0192 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

/**
   * Creates a new pass for analyzing prototype properties.
   * @param compiler The compiler.
   * @param moduleGraph The graph for resolving module dependencies. May be
   *     null if we don't care about module dependencies.
   * @param canModifyExterns If true, then we can move prototype
   *     properties that are declared in the externs file.
   * @param anchorUnusedVars If true, then we must mark all vars as referenced,
   *     even if they are never used.
   */
Method code:
114 |   AnalyzePrototypeProperties(AbstractCompiler compiler,

===== M0210 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.setSourceInfo(Node,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 296-313
Documentation:

Method code:
296 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
297 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
298 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
299 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
300 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
301 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
302 |     } else {
303 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
304 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
305 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
306 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
307 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
308 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
309 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
310 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
311 |       }
312 |     }
313 |   }

===== M0230 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0233 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 832-874
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
832 |   @Override
833 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
834 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
835 |       return true;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
839 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
840 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
841 |         return true;
842 |       }
843 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
844 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
845 |         return false;
846 |       }
847 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
848 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
849 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
850 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
851 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
852 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
853 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
854 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
855 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
856 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
857 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
858 | 
859 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
860 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
861 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
862 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
863 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
864 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
865 | 
866 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
867 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
868 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
869 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
870 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
874 |   }

===== M0234 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 441-456
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

===== M0241 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1005-1075
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1005 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
1006 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
1007 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
1008 |                           String backslashEscape,
1009 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
1010 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1011 |     sb.append(quote);
1012 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1013 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1014 |       switch (c) {
1015 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\0"); break;
1016 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1017 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1018 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1019 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1020 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1021 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1022 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1023 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1024 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1025 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1026 |             sb.append("\\>");
1027 |           } else {
1028 |             sb.append(c);
1029 |           }
1030 |           break;
1031 |         case '<':
1032 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1033 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1034 | 
1035 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1036 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1037 | 
1038 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1039 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1040 |             sb.append("<\\");
1041 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1042 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1043 |             sb.append("<\\");
1044 |           } else {
1045 |             sb.append(c);
1046 |           }
1047 |           break;
1048 |         default:
1049 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1050 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1051 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1052 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1053 |               sb.append(c);
1054 |             } else {
1055 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1056 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1057 |             }
1058 |           } else {
1059 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1060 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1061 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1062 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1063 |               sb.append(c);
1064 |             } else {
1065 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1066 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1067 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1068 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1069 |             }
1070 |           }
1071 |       }
1072 |     }
1073 |     sb.append(quote);
1074 |     return sb.toString();
1075 |   }

===== M0251 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 582-639
Documentation:

Method code:
582 |     @Override
583 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
584 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
585 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
586 |       if (name == null) {
587 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
588 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
589 |           errorReporter.error(
590 |             "unnamed function statement",
591 |             sourceName,
592 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
593 |         }
594 |         name = new Name();
595 |         name.setIdentifier("");
596 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
597 |       }
598 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
599 |       Node newName = transform(name);
600 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
601 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
602 |         // declaration.
603 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
604 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
605 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
606 |         // same line as the function keyword.
607 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
608 |             functionNode.getLp();
609 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
610 |       }
611 | 
612 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
613 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
614 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
615 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
616 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
617 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
618 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
619 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
620 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
621 |       if (fnName != null) {
622 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
623 |       } else {
624 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
625 |       }
626 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
627 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
628 | 
629 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
630 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
631 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
632 |       }
633 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
634 | 
635 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
636 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
637 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
638 |      return node;
639 |     }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
478 |             unknown = true;
479 |             break;
480 |           }
481 |         }
482 |       } else {
483 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
484 |       }
485 |     }
486 |     return unknown;
487 |   }

===== M0275 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 380-393
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
381 |       Node ret;
382 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
383 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
384 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
385 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
386 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
387 |       } else {
388 |         ret = transform(n);
389 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
390 |       }
391 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
392 |       return ret;
393 |     }

Method code:
781 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
782 |     boolean valid = false;
783 |     if (i == 1) {
784 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
785 |     } else if (i == 2) {

789 |     }
790 |

794 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);

===== M0294 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 420-436
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
420 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
421 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
422 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
423 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
424 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
425 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
426 |         String inputName = input.getName();
427 | 
428 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
429 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
430 |         // will get an error down the line.
431 |         inputs.add(input);
432 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
433 |       }
434 |     }
435 |     return inputs;
436 |   }

652 |     @Override
653 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
654 |       Node n =  newNode(
655 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
656 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
657 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
658 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
659 |       // doesn't do.
660 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
661 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned

663 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
664 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
665 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
666 |       return n;
667 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 456-483
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

Method code:
153 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
154 |     switch (n.getType()) {
155 |       // Childless expressions
156 |       case Token.FALSE:
157 |       case Token.NULL:
158 |       case Token.THIS:
159 |       case Token.TRUE:
160 |         validateChildless(n);
161 |         return;
162 | 
163 |       // General uniary ops
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.POS:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.INC:
169 |       case Token.DEC:
170 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
171 |       case Token.VOID:
172 |       case Token.BITNOT:
173 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
174 |         return;
175 | 
176 |       // General binary ops
177 |       case Token.COMMA:
178 |       case Token.OR:
179 |       case Token.AND:
180 |       case Token.BITOR:
181 |       case Token.BITXOR:
182 |       case Token.BITAND:
183 |       case Token.EQ:
184 |       case Token.NE:
185 |       case Token.SHEQ:
186 |       case Token.SHNE:
187 |       case Token.LT:
188 |       case Token.GT:
189 |       case Token.LE:
190 |       case Token.GE:
191 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
192 |       case Token.IN:
193 |       case Token.LSH:
194 |       case Token.RSH:
195 |       case Token.URSH:
196 |       case Token.SUB:
197 |       case Token.ADD:
198 |       case Token.MUL:
199 |       case Token.MOD:
200 |       case Token.DIV:
201 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
202 |         return;
203 | 
204 |       // Assignments
205 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
217 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
218 |         return;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.HOOK:
221 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
222 |         return;
223 | 
224 |       // Node types that require special handling
225 |       case Token.STRING:
226 |         validateString(n);
227 |         return;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.NUMBER:
230 |         validateNumber(n);
231 |         return;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.NAME:
234 |         validateName(n);
235 |         return;
236 | 
237 |       case Token.GETELEM:
238 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
239 |         return;
240 | 
241 |       case Token.GETPROP:
242 |         validateGetProp(n);
243 |         return;
244 | 
245 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
246 |         validateArrayLit(n);
247 |         return;
248 | 
249 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
250 |         validateObjectLit(n);
251 |         return;
252 | 
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
255 |         return;
256 | 
257 |       case Token.CALL:
258 |         validateCall(n);
259 |         return;
260 | 
261 |       case Token.NEW:
262 |         validateNew(n);
263 |         return;
264 | 
265 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
266 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
267 |         return;
268 | 
269 |       default:
270 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
271 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()), n);
272 |     }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1891-1905
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1891 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1892 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1893 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1894 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1895 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1896 |       if (result.last != null) {
1897 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1898 |       }
1899 |       if (result.first == null) {
1900 |         result.first = n2clone;
1901 |       }
1902 |       result.last = n2clone;
1903 |     }
1904 |     return result;
1905 |   }

===== M0349 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 436-488
Documentation:

Method code:
436 |   @Override
437 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
438 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
439 |       return true;
440 |     }
441 | 
442 |     // Union types
443 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
444 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
445 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
446 |       return false;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // record types
450 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
451 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // Interfaces
455 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
456 |     // to the interface instance.
457 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
458 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
459 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
460 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
461 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
462 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
463 |           return true;
464 |         }
465 |       }
466 |     }
467 | 
468 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
469 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
470 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
471 |           return true;
472 |         }
473 |       }
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     // other prototype based objects
477 |     if (that != null) {
478 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
479 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
480 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
481 |         // to avoid guessing.
482 |         return true;
483 |       }
484 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     return false;
488 |   }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2449-2459
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2449 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2450 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2451 |     int len = s.length();
2452 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2453 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2454 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2455 |         return false;
2456 |       }
2457 |     }
2458 |     return true;
2459 |   }

===== M0367 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AnalyzePrototypeProperties.ProcessProperties.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AnalyzePrototypeProperties.java
Original range: 220-286
Documentation:

Method code:
220 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
221 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
222 |         String propName = n.getFirstChild().getNext().getString();
223 |         if (propName.equals("prototype")) {
224 |           processPrototypeParent(t, parent);
225 |         } else if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(propName)) {
226 |           addGlobalUseOfSymbol(propName, t.getModule(), PROPERTY);
227 |         } else {
228 |           addSymbolUse(propName, t.getModule(), PROPERTY);
229 |         }
230 |       } else if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT &&
231 |           // Make sure that we're not handling object literals being
232 |           // assigned to a prototype, as in:
233 |           // Foo.prototype = {bar: 3, baz: 5};
234 |           !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
235 |             parent.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
236 |             parent.getFirstChild().getLastChild().getString().equals(
237 |                 "prototype"))) {
238 |         // var x = {a: 1, b: 2}
239 |         // should count as a use of property a and b.
240 |         for (Node propNameNode = n.getFirstChild(); propNameNode != null;
241 |              propNameNode = propNameNode.getNext()) {
242 |           // May be STRING, GET, or SET, but NUMBER isn't interesting.
243 |           if (!propNameNode.isQuotedString()) {
244 |             addSymbolUse(propNameNode.getString(), t.getModule(), PROPERTY);
245 |           }
246 |         }
247 |       } else if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
248 |         String name = n.getString();
249 | 
250 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
251 |         if (var != null) {
252 |           // Only process global functions.
253 |           if (var.isGlobal()) {
254 |             if (var.getInitialValue() != null &&
255 |                 var.getInitialValue().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
256 |               if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
257 |                 if (!processGlobalFunctionDeclaration(t, n, parent,
258 |                         parent.getParent())) {
259 |                   addGlobalUseOfSymbol(name, t.getModule(), VAR);
260 |                 }
261 |               } else {
262 |                 addSymbolUse(name, t.getModule(), VAR);
263 |               }
264 |             }
265 | 
266 |           // If it is not a global, it might be accessing a local of the outer
267 |           // scope. If that's the case the functions between the variable's
268 |           // declaring scope and the variable reference scope cannot be moved.
269 |           } else if (var.getScope() != t.getScope()){
270 |             for (int i = symbolStack.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
271 |               NameContext context = symbolStack.get(i);
272 |               context.name.readClosureVariables = true;
273 |               if (context.scope == var.getScope()) {
274 |                 break;
275 |               }
276 |             }
277 |           }
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 | 
281 |       if (isPrototypePropertyAssign(n) ||
282 |           isGlobalFunctionDeclaration(t, n) ||
283 |           NodeUtil.isFunction(n)) {
284 |         symbolStack.pop();
285 |       }
286 |     }

509 |       if (objType != null) {
510 |         if (objType.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
511 |           return true;
512 |         }
513 |         return setPrototype(
514 |             new FunctionPrototypeType(
515 |                 registry, this, objType, isNativeObjectType()));
516 |       } else {
517 |         return false;
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return super.defineProperty(name, type, inferred, inExterns, propertyNode);
521 |   }

===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()

Original range: 459-475
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
447 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {

449 |          current != null;

451 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {

453 |       }
454 |     }
455 |     return false;
456 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 433-453
Documentation:

Method code:
433 |     @Override
434 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
435 |       if (lineBreak) {
436 |         if (sawFunction) {
437 |           startNewLine();
438 |           sawFunction = false;
439 |         }
440 |       }
441 | 
442 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
443 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
444 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
445 |       int len = code.length();

448 |         if (ch == ';') {
449 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
450 |         }
451 |       }

453 |     }

===== M0416 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1924-1926
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1924 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1925 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1926 |   }

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1091-1229
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1091 |   Node parseInputs() {
1092 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1093 | 
1094 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1095 |     // individual file parse trees.
1096 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1097 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1098 |     }
1099 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1100 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1101 |     }
1102 | 
1103 |     // Parse main js sources.
1104 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1105 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1106 | 
1107 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1108 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1109 | 
1110 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1111 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1112 | 
1113 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1114 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1115 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1116 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1117 |     }
1118 | 
1119 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1120 | 
1121 |     try {
1122 |       // Parse externs sources.
1123 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1124 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1125 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1126 |           return null;
1127 |         }
1128 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1132 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1133 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1134 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1135 | 
1136 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1137 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1138 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1139 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1140 |           }
1141 |         }
1142 | 
1143 |         try {
1144 |           inputs =
1145 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1146 |               .manageDependencies(
1147 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1148 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1149 |           report(JSError.make(
1150 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1151 |           return null;
1152 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1153 |           report(JSError.make(
1154 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1155 |           return null;
1156 |         }
1157 |       }
1158 | 
1159 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1160 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1161 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1162 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1163 | 
1164 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1165 |         if (n == null) {
1166 |           continue;
1167 |         }
1168 | 
1169 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1170 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1171 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1172 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1173 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1174 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1175 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1176 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1177 | 
1178 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1179 | 
1180 |             externs.add(input);
1181 |             staleInputs = true;
1182 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1183 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1184 |             staleInputs = true;
1185 |           }
1186 |         }
1187 |       }
1188 | 
1189 |       if (staleInputs) {
1190 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1191 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1192 |       }
1193 | 
1194 |       // Build the AST.
1195 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1196 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1197 |         if (n == null) {
1198 |           continue;
1199 |         }
1200 | 
1201 |         if (devMode) {
1202 |           runSanityCheck();
1203 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1204 |             return null;
1205 |           }
1206 |         }
1207 | 
1208 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1209 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1210 | 
1211 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1212 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1213 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1214 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1215 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1216 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1217 |         }
1218 | 
1219 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1220 |       }
1221 | 
1222 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1223 |         return null;
1224 |       }
1225 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1226 |     } finally {
1227 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1228 |     }
1229 |   }

===== M0423 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }
