===== M0426 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1740-1793
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1740 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1741 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1742 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1743 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1744 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1745 |       } else {
1746 |         // Otherwise only its children can be removed.
1747 |         node.detachChildren();
1748 |       }
1749 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.CATCH) {
1750 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1751 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1752 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1753 |       node.detachFromParent();
1754 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1755 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1756 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1757 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1758 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1759 |       node.detachChildren();
1760 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1761 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1762 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1763 |       node.detachChildren();
1764 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1765 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1766 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1767 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1768 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
1769 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1770 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1771 |       } else {
1772 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1773 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1774 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1775 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1776 |       }
1777 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LABEL
1778 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1779 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1780 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1781 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1782 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1783 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.FOR
1784 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1785 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1786 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1787 |       // or the structure removed.
1788 |       parent.replaceChild(node, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1789 |     } else {
1790 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1791 |           node.toString() + " of "+ parent.toString());
1792 |     }
1793 |   }

===== M0432 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1162-1170
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1162 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1163 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1164 |       return -1;
1165 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1166 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1167 |     } else {
1168 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1169 |     }
1170 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 956-983
Documentation:

Method code:
956 |   String jsString(String s) {
957 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
958 | 
959 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
960 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
961 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
962 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
963 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
964 |       }
965 |     }
966 | 
967 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
968 |     char quote;
969 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
970 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
971 |       quote = '\'';
972 |       doublequote = "\"";
973 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
974 |     } else {
975 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
976 |       quote = '\"';
977 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
978 |       singlequote = "\'";
979 |     }
980 | 
981 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
982 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
983 |   }

===== M0474 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 86-735
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(
100 |           childCount == 2,
101 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
102 |           opstr, childCount);
103 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
104 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
105 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
106 | 
107 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
108 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
109 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
110 | 
111 |       // Handle associativity.
112 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
113 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
114 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
115 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
118 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
119 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
120 |       } else {
121 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
122 |       }
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
127 | 
128 |     switch (type) {
129 |       case Token.TRY: {
130 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
131 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
132 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
133 | 
134 |         add("try");
135 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 | 
137 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
138 |         // isn't a catch block
139 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
140 |         if (catchblock != null) {
141 |           add(catchblock);
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         if (childCount == 3) {
145 |           add("finally");
146 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
147 |         }
148 |         break;
149 |       }
150 | 
151 |       case Token.CATCH:
152 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
153 |         add("catch(");
154 |         add(first);
155 |         add(")");
156 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
157 |         break;
158 | 
159 |       case Token.THROW:
160 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
161 |         add("throw");
162 |         add(first);
163 | 
164 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
165 |         // parse this.
166 |         cc.endStatement(true);
167 |         break;
168 | 
169 |       case Token.RETURN:
170 |         add("return");
171 |         if (childCount == 1) {
172 |           add(first);
173 |         } else {
174 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
175 |         }
176 |         cc.endStatement();
177 |         break;
178 | 
179 |       case Token.VAR:
180 |         if (first != null) {
181 |           add("var ");
182 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 | 
186 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
187 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
188 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
189 |         break;
190 | 
191 |       case Token.NAME:
192 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |         } else {
195 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
196 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
197 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
198 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
199 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
200 |           } else {
201 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
202 |             // precedence.
203 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
204 |           }
205 |         }
206 |         break;
207 | 
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |         add("[");
210 |         addArrayList(first);
211 |         add("]");
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.LP:
215 |         add("(");
216 |         addList(first);
217 |         add(")");
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.COMMA:
221 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
222 |         addList(first, false, context);
223 |         break;
224 | 
225 |       case Token.NUMBER:
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
227 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
231 |       case Token.VOID:
232 |       case Token.NOT:
233 |       case Token.BITNOT:
234 |       case Token.POS: {
235 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
237 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
238 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
239 |         break;
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       case Token.NEG: {
243 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
244 | 
245 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
246 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
247 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
248 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
249 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
250 |         } else {
251 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
252 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |         break;
256 |       }
257 | 
258 |       case Token.HOOK: {
259 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
260 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
261 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
262 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
263 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
264 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
265 |         addExpr(last, 1);
266 |         break;
267 |       }
268 | 
269 |       case Token.REGEXP:
270 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
271 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
272 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
276 | 
277 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
278 |         if (childCount == 2) {
279 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
280 |         } else {
281 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
282 |           add(regexp);
283 |         }
284 |         break;
285 | 
286 |       case Token.GET_REF:
287 |         add(first);
288 |         break;
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
292 |         add(first);
293 |         add(".");
294 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
295 |         break;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
298 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
299 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
300 |         }
301 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
302 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
303 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
304 |           add("(");
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         add("function");
308 |         add(first);
309 | 
310 |         add(first.getNext());
311 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
312 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
313 | 
314 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
315 |           add(")");
316 |         }
317 |         break;
318 | 
319 |       case Token.GET:
320 |       case Token.SET:
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
323 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
324 | 
325 |         // Get methods are unnamed
326 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
327 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
328 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
329 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
330 |           add("get ");
331 |         } else {
332 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
333 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
334 |           add("set ");
335 |         }
336 | 
337 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
338 |         String name = n.getString();
339 |         Node fn = first;
340 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
341 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
342 | 
343 |         // Add the property name.
344 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
345 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
346 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
347 |             // unicode escaped.
348 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
349 |           add(name);
350 |         } else {
351 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
352 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
353 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
354 |             cc.addNumber(d);
355 |           } else {
356 |             addJsString(n.getString());
357 |           }
358 |         }
359 | 
360 |         add(parameters);
361 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
362 |         break;
363 | 
364 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
365 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
366 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
367 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
368 |         }
369 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
370 |         if (preserveBlock) {
371 |           cc.beginBlock();
372 |         }
373 | 
374 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
375 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
376 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
377 |                 !preserveBlock &&
378 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
379 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
380 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
381 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
382 | 
383 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
384 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
385 |             cc.endStatement();
386 |           }
387 | 
388 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
389 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
390 |           }
391 | 
392 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
393 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
394 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
395 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
396 |           }
397 |         }
398 |         if (preserveBlock) {
399 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
400 |         }
401 |         break;
402 |       }
403 | 
404 |       case Token.FOR:
405 |         if (childCount == 4) {
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
408 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
409 |           } else {
410 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
411 |           }
412 |           add(";");
413 |           add(first.getNext());
414 |           add(";");
415 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
416 |           add(")");
417 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
418 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
419 |         } else {
420 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
421 |           add("for(");
422 |           add(first);
423 |           add("in");
424 |           add(first.getNext());
425 |           add(")");
426 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
427 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.DO:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("do");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(last);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         cc.endStatement();
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.WHILE:
442 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(first);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
447 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.EMPTY:
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
455 |         Preconditions.checkState(
456 |             childCount == 2,
457 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
458 |         Preconditions.checkState(
459 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
460 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
461 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
462 |         if (needsParens) {
463 |           add("(");
464 |         }
465 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
466 |         if (needsParens) {
467 |           add(")");
468 |         }
469 |         add(".");
470 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
471 |         break;
472 |       }
473 | 
474 |       case Token.GETELEM:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(
476 |             childCount == 2,
477 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
478 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
479 |         add("[");
480 |         add(first.getNext());
481 |         add("]");
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.WITH:
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
486 |         add("with(");
487 |         add(first);
488 |         add(")");
489 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
490 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.INC:
494 |       case Token.DEC: {
495 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
496 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
497 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
498 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
499 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
500 |         if (postProp != 0) {
501 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
502 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
503 |         } else {
504 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
505 |           add(first);
506 |         }
507 |         break;
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       case Token.CALL:
511 |         // We have two special cases here:
512 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
513 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
515 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
516 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
517 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
518 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
519 |         // that must be preserved.
520 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
521 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
522 |           add("(0,");
523 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
524 |           add(")");
525 |         } else {
526 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
527 |         }
528 |         add("(");
529 |         addList(first.getNext());
530 |         add(")");
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.IF:
534 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
535 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
536 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
537 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
538 |           cc.beginBlock();
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         add("if(");
542 |         add(first);
543 |         add(")");
544 | 
545 |         if (hasElse) {
546 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
547 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
548 |           add("else");
549 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
550 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
551 |         } else {
552 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
553 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
554 |         }
555 | 
556 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
557 |           cc.endBlock();
558 |         }
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.NULL:
562 |       case Token.THIS:
563 |       case Token.FALSE:
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
602 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
603 | 
604 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
605 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
606 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
607 |         cc.endStatement();
608 |         break;
609 | 
610 |       case Token.NEW:
611 |         add("new ");
612 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
613 | 
614 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
615 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
616 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
617 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }

===== M0477 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 55-73
Documentation:

Method code:
55 |   @Override
56 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
57 |     if (checkOnly) {
58 |       normalizeNodeTypes(root);
59 |     } else {
60 |       // Don't perform "PrepareAnnotations" when doing checks as
61 |       // they currently aren't valid during sanity checks.  In particular,
62 |       // they DIRECT_EVAL shouldn't be applied after inlining has been
63 |       // performed.
64 |       if (externs != null) {
65 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
66 |             compiler, externs, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
67 |       }
68 |       if (root != null) {
69 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
70 |             compiler, root, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
71 |       }
72 |     }
73 |   }

485 |         JSTypeNative.STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
486 | 
487 |     // (Number, number)
488 |     JSType NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE =
489 |         createUnionType(NUMBER_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE);
490 |     registerNativeType(
491 |         JSTypeNative.NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
492 | 
493 |     // unknown function type, i.e. (?...) -> ?
494 |     FunctionType U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE =
495 |         createFunctionType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE);
496 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE, U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE);
497 | 
498 |     // unknown constructor type, i.e. (?...) -> ? with the NoObject type
499 |     // as instance type
500 |     FunctionType U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE =
501 |         // This is equivalent to
502 |         // createConstructorType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE), but,
503 |         // in addition, overrides getInstanceType() to return the NoObject type
504 |         // instead of a new anonymous object.
505 |         new FunctionType(this, "Function", null,
506 |             createArrowType(
507 |                 createParametersWithVarArgs(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
508 |                 UNKNOWN_TYPE),
509 |             NO_OBJECT_TYPE, null, true, true) {
510 |           private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
511 | 
512 |           @Override public FunctionType getConstructor() {
513 |             return registry.getNativeFunctionType(
514 |                 JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
515 |           }
516 |         };
517 | 
518 |     // The U2U_CONSTRUCTOR is weird, because it's the supertype of its
519 |     // own constructor.
520 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
521 |     registerNativeType(
522 |         JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
523 | 
524 |     FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE.setInstanceType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
525 |     U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE.setImplicitPrototype(FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE);
526 | 
527 |     // least function type, i.e. (All...) -> NoType
528 |     FunctionType LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
529 |         createFunctionType(NO_TYPE, true, ALL_TYPE);
530 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE, LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
531 | 
532 |     // the 'this' object in the global scope
533 |     FunctionType GLOBAL_THIS_CTOR =
534 |         new FunctionType(this, "global this", null,
535 |             createArrowType(createParameters(false, ALL_TYPE), NUMBER_TYPE),
536 |             null, null, true, true);
537 |     ObjectType GLOBAL_THIS = GLOBAL_THIS_CTOR.getInstanceType();
538 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS, GLOBAL_THIS);
539 | 
540 |     // greatest function type, i.e. (NoType...) -> All
541 |     FunctionType GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
542 |       createFunctionType(ALL_TYPE, true, NO_TYPE);
543 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE,
544 |         GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
545 |

547 |     // registerPropertyOnType about the bootstrapping process.
548 |     registerPropertyOnType("prototype", OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE);
549 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2851 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2852 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2853 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2854 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2855 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2856 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2857 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2858 |         // on ASSIGNs
2859 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2860 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2861 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2862 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2863 |       }
2864 |     }
2865 |     return fnInfo;
2866 |   }

===== M0486 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-282
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
241 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
242 |       options.checkTypes = true;
243 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = false;
245 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
246 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
247 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
248 |       // parser type warnings.
249 |       options.setWarningLevel(
250 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
251 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
252 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
256 |       options.setWarningLevel(
257 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
258 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
262 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
263 |     guards.add(
264 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
265 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
266 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
267 | 
268 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
269 | 
270 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
271 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
272 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
273 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
274 |     // error off.
275 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
276 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
277 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
282 |   }

Documentation:
/**
     * Processes a NAME node to see if it's a global function declaration.
     * If it is, record it and return true. Otherwise, return false.
     */
Method code:
340 |     private boolean processGlobalFunctionDeclaration(NodeTraversal t,
341 |         Node nameNode, Node parent, Node gramps) {
342 |       Node firstChild = nameNode.getFirstChild();
343 | 
344 |       if (// Check for a named FUNCTION.
345 |           isGlobalFunctionDeclaration(t, parent) ||
346 |           // Check for a VAR declaration.
347 |           firstChild != null &&
348 |           isGlobalFunctionDeclaration(t, firstChild)) {
349 |         String name = nameNode.getString();
350 |         getNameInfoForName(name, VAR).getDeclarations().add(
351 |             new GlobalFunction(nameNode, parent, gramps, t.getModule()));
352 | 
353 |         // If the function name is exported, we should create an edge here
354 |         // so that it's never removed.
355 |         if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(name) ||
356 |             anchorUnusedVars) {
357 |           addGlobalUseOfSymbol(name, t.getModule(), VAR);
358 |         }
359 | 
360 |         return true;
361 |       }
362 |       return false;
363 |     }

===== M0517 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 304-336
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
304 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
305 |     if (prototype == null) {
306 |       return false;
307 |     }
308 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
309 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
310 |       return false;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
314 |     this.prototype = prototype;
315 | 
316 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
317 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
318 |       if (superClass != null) {
319 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       if (isInterface()) {
323 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
324 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
325 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
326 |           }
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
332 |       clearCachedValues();
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     return true;
336 |   }

/**
   * Returns true if the operator is associative.
   * e.g. (a * b) * c = a * (b * c)
   * Note: "+" is not associative because it is also the concatenation
   * for strings. e.g. "a" + (1 + 2) is not "a" + 1 + 2
   */
Method code:
1343 |   static boolean isAssociative(int type) {
1344 |     switch (type) {
1345 |       case Token.MUL:
1346 |       case Token.AND:
1347 |       case Token.OR:
1348 |       case Token.BITOR:
1349 |       case Token.BITXOR:
1350 |       case Token.BITAND:
1351 |         return true;
1352 |       default:
1353 |         return false;
1354 |     }
1355 |   }

===== M0524 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 555-569
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
563 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
564 |       scopes.push(scope);
565 |     }
566 |     scopeRoots.clear();
567 | 
568 |     return scope;
569 |   }

===== M0525 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1158-1169
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

===== M0579 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0582 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AnalyzePrototypeProperties.ProcessProperties.processPrototypeParent(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AnalyzePrototypeProperties.java
Original range: 370-402
Documentation:
/**
     * Processes the parent of a GETPROP prototype, which can either be
     * another GETPROP (in the case of Foo.prototype.bar), or can be
     * an assignment (in the case of Foo.prototype = ...).
     */
Method code:
370 |     private void processPrototypeParent(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
371 |       switch (n.getType()) {
372 |         // Foo.prototype.getBar = function() { ... }
373 |         case Token.GETPROP:
374 |           Node dest = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
375 |           Node parent = n.getParent();
376 |           Node grandParent = parent.getParent();
377 | 
378 |           if (dest.getType() == Token.STRING &&
379 |               NodeUtil.isExprAssign(grandParent) &&
380 |               NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(n, parent)) {
381 |             String name = dest.getString();
382 |             Property prop = new AssignmentProperty(grandParent, t.getModule());
383 |             getNameInfoForName(name, PROPERTY).getDeclarations().add(prop);
384 |           }
385 |           break;
386 | 
387 |         // Foo.prototype = { "getBar" : function() { ... } }
388 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
389 |           Node map = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
390 |           if (map.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
391 |             for (Node key = map.getFirstChild();
392 |                  key != null; key = key.getNext()) {
393 |               // May be STRING, GET, or SET,
394 |               String name = key.getString();
395 |               Property prop = new LiteralProperty(
396 |                   key, key.getFirstChild(), map, n, t.getModule());
397 |               getNameInfoForName(name, PROPERTY).getDeclarations().add(prop);
398 |             }
399 |           }
400 |           break;
401 |       }

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,int,SourceMap,SourceMap.DetailLevel,Charset,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 609-644
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
609 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
610 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
611 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
612 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
613 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
614 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {
615 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
616 | 
617 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
618 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
619 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
620 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
621 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
622 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
623 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
624 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
625 |     CodeGenerator cg =
626 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
627 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
628 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
629 | 
630 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
631 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     cg.add(root);
635 |     mcp.endFile();
636 | 
637 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
638 | 
639 |     if (createSourceMap) {
640 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     return code;
644 |   }
